STEP 62 CALL 1 RETURN 0
COMMAND:
cd /app && cat > __tests__/linter-disable-smoke.test.ts <<'EOF'
import {RulesRunner, createRunLinterRulesOptions} from '../src/rules-runner';
import {DEFAULT_SETTINGS, LinterSettings} from '../src/settings-data';
import {rules} from '../src/rules';
import {getLinterDisableDirectives, getLinterDisableSectionsInText} from '../src/utils/linter-disable';
import '../src/rules-registry';
import {defaultMisspellings} from './common';
import dedent from 'ts-dedent';

const rulesRunner = new RulesRunner();

function lint(text: string, enabledRules: string[]): string {
  const settings = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(DEFAULT_SETTINGS)) as LinterSettings;
  settings.ruleConfigs = {};
  for (const rule of rules) {
    settings.ruleConfigs[rule.alias] = {};
  }
  for (const alias of enabledRules) {
    settings.ruleConfigs[alias] = {enabled: true};
  }
  const options = createRunLinterRulesOptions(text, null, 'en-gb', settings, defaultMisspellings());
  return rulesRunner.lintText(options);
}

describe('linter disable integration', () => {
  it('per-rule disable prevents only that rule', () => {
    const before = dedent`
      Some text   
      <!-- linter-disable: trailing-spaces -->
      Ignored text   
      <!-- linter-enable: trailing-spaces -->
      More text   
    `;
    const after = lint(before, ['trailing-spaces']);
    expect(after).toContain('Ignored text   ');
    expect(after).toContain('<!-- linter-disable: trailing-spaces -->');
    expect(after).toContain('<!-- linter-enable: trailing-spaces -->');
    expect(after).not.toContain('Some text   ');
    expect(after).not.toContain('More text   ');
  });

  it('marker lines never modified for other rule', () => {
    const before = dedent`
      <!-- linter-disable: trailing-spaces -->
      a
      <!-- linter-enable: trailing-spaces -->
      b   
    `;
    const after = lint(before, ['trailing-spaces']);
    expect(after).toContain('<!-- linter-disable: trailing-spaces -->');
    expect(after).toContain('<!-- linter-enable: trailing-spaces -->');
  });

  it('disable-next-line', () => {
    const before = dedent`
      a   
      <!-- linter-disable-next-line: trailing-spaces -->
      b   
      c   
    `;
    const after = lint(before, ['trailing-spaces']);
    expect(after).toContain('b   ');
    expect(after).not.toContain('c   ');
    expect(after).toContain('<!-- linter-disable-next-line: trailing-spaces -->');
  });

  it('disable-next-n-lines', () => {
    const before = dedent`
      a   
      <!-- linter-disable-next-n-lines: 2 trailing-spaces -->
      b   
      c   
      d   
    `;
    const after = lint(before, ['trailing-spaces']);
    expect(after).toContain('b   ');
    expect(after).toContain('c   ');
    expect(after).not.toContain('d   ');
  });

  it('all disable re-enable specific rule within scope', () => {
    const before = dedent`
      a   
      <!-- linter-disable -->
      b   
      <!-- linter-enable: trailing-spaces -->
      c   
      <!-- linter-enable -->
      d   
    `;
    const after = lint(before, ['trailing-spaces']);
    expect(after).toContain('b   ');
    expect(after).not.toContain('c   ');
    expect(after).not.toContain('d   ');
  });

  it('markers in code blocks are ignored', () => {
    const before = dedent`
      a   
      \`\`\`
      <!-- linter-disable: trailing-spaces -->
      b   
      <!-- linter-enable: trailing-spaces -->
      \`\`\`
      c   
    `;
    const after = lint(before, ['trailing-spaces']);
    // code content unchanged (code ignored by rule), but c should be linted
    expect(after).toContain('<!-- linter-disable: trailing-spaces -->');
    expect(after).not.toContain('c   ');
  });

  it('markers not on standalone line are ignored', () => {
    const before = dedent`
      a <!-- linter-disable: trailing-spaces -->   
      b   
    `;
    const after = lint(before, ['trailing-spaces']);
    expect(after).not.toContain('b   ');
  });

  it('unknown rules marker has no effect', () => {
    const before = dedent`
      a   
      <!-- linter-disable: not-a-rule -->
      b   
    `;
    const after = lint(before, ['trailing-spaces']);
    expect(after).not.toContain('b   ');
  });

  it('case insensitive and duplicate removal', () => {
    const text = dedent`
      <!-- linter-disable: Trailing-Spaces, TRAILING-SPACES, , -->
      a
    `;
    const directives = getLinterDisableDirectives(text);
    const d = directives.find(x => x.type === 'disable');
    expect([...d.rules]).toEqual(['trailing-spaces']);
  });

  it('nested scopes with stack enable', () => {
    const before = dedent`
      a   
      <!-- linter-disable -->
      b   
      <!-- linter-disable: trailing-spaces -->
      c   
      <!-- linter-enable -->
      d   
      <!-- linter-enable -->
      e   
    `;
    const after = lint(before, ['trailing-spaces']);
    // a linted, b disabled (all), c disabled (all), d disabled by outer all, e linted
    expect(after).not.toContain('a   ');
    expect(after).toContain('b   ');
    expect(after).toContain('c   ');
    expect(after).toContain('d   ');
    expect(after).not.toContain('e   ');
  });

  it('sections for a rule that is not disabled are just marker lines', () => {
    const text = dedent`
      <!-- linter-disable: trailing-spaces -->
      a
      <!-- linter-enable: trailing-spaces -->
      b
    `;
    const sections = getLinterDisableSectionsInText(text, 'capitalize-headings');
    expect(sections.length).toBe(2);
    expect(text.substring(sections[0].startIndex, sections[0].endIndex)).toContain('linter-disable');
  });
});
EOF

import dedent from 'ts-dedent';

class TrailingSpacesOptions implements Options {
  twoSpaceLineBreak: boolean = false;
}

@RuleBuilder.register
export default class TrailingSpaces extends RuleBuilder<TrailingSpacesOptions> {
  constructor() {
    super({
      nameKey: 'rules.trailing-spaces.name',
      descriptionKey: 'rules.trailing-spaces.description',
      type: RuleType.SPACING,
      hasSpecialExecutionOrder: true, // run after all other possible rules to make sure trailing spaces are properly removed
      ruleIgnoreTypes: [IgnoreTypes.code, IgnoreTypes.math, IgnoreTypes.yaml, IgnoreTypes.link, IgnoreTypes.wikiLink, IgnoreTypes.tag],
    });
  }
  get OptionsClass(): new () => TrailingSpacesOptions {
    return TrailingSpacesOptions;
  }
  apply(text: string, options: TrailingSpacesOptions): string {
    text = ignoreListOfTypes([IgnoreTypes.list], text, (text: string): string => {
      if (!options.twoSpaceLineBreak) {
        return text.replace(/[ \t]+$/gm, '');
      } else {
        text = text.replace(/(\S)[ \t]$/gm, '$1'); // one whitespace
        text = text.replace(/(\S)[ \t]{3,}$/gm, '$1'); // three or more whitespaces
        text = text.replace(/(\S)( ?\t\t? ?)$/gm, '$1'); // two whitespaces with at least one tab
        return text;
      }
    });

    return updateListItemText(text, (text: string): string => {
      if (!options.twoSpaceLineBreak) {
        return text.replace(/[ \t]+$/gm, '');

cd /app && cat >> __tests__/linter-disable-smoke.test.ts <<'EOF'

describe('linter disable edge cases', () => {
  it('marker line preserved for a rule it does not disable', () => {
    const before = dedent`
      <!-- linter-disable: capitalize-headings -->
      a   
    `;
    const after = lint(before, ['trailing-spaces']);
    expect(after).toContain('<!-- linter-disable: capitalize-headings -->');
  });

  it('markers inside YAML frontmatter are ignored', () => {
    const before = dedent`
      ---
      <!-- linter-disable: trailing-spaces -->
      ---
      a   
    `;
    const after = lint(before, ['trailing-spaces']);
    expect(after).not.toContain('a   ');
  });

  it('markers inside math block are ignored', () => {
    const before = dedent`
      $$
      <!-- linter-disable: trailing-spaces -->
      $$
      a   
    `;
    const after = lint(before, ['trailing-spaces']);
    expect(after).not.toContain('a   ');
  });

  it('markers inside inline code are ignored', () => {
    const before = "`a\n<!-- linter-disable: trailing-spaces -->\nz`\n  b   \n";
    const after = lint(before, ['trailing-spaces']);
    expect(after).not.toContain('b   ');
  });

  it('obsidian markers work', () => {
    const before = dedent`
      a   
      %% linter-disable: trailing-spaces %%
      b   
      %% linter-enable: trailing-spaces %%
      c   
    `;
    const after = lint(before, ['trailing-spaces']);
    expect(after).toContain('b   ');
    expect(after).not.toContain('c   ');
    expect(after).toContain('%% linter-disable: trailing-spaces %%');
    expect(after).toContain('%% linter-enable: trailing-spaces %%');
  });

  it('disable-next-n-lines clamps to EOF', () => {
    const before = dedent`
      a   
      <!-- linter-disable-next-n-lines: 5 trailing-spaces -->
      b   
      c   
    `;
    const after = lint(before, ['trailing-spaces']);
    expect(after).toContain('b   ');
    expect(after).toContain('c   ');
  });

  it('disable-next-line at EOF has no effect', () => {
    const before = `a   \n<!-- linter-disable-next-line: trailing-spaces -->\n`;
    const after = lint(before, ['trailing-spaces']);
    expect(after).not.toContain('a   ');
  });

  it('invalid N has no effect', () => {
    const before = dedent`
      a   
      <!-- linter-disable-next-n-lines: 0 trailing-spaces -->
      b   
      <!-- linter-disable-next-n-lines: -1 trailing-spaces -->
      c   
      <!-- linter-disable-next-n-lines: abc trailing-spaces -->
      d   
    `;
    const after = lint(before, ['trailing-spaces']);
    expect(after).not.toContain('b   ');
    expect(after).not.toContain('c   ');
    expect(after).not.toContain('d   ');
  });

  it('empty rule list has no effect', () => {
    const before = dedent`
      a   
      <!-- linter-disable: ,,, -->
      b   
    `;
    const after = lint(before, ['trailing-spaces']);
    expect(after).not.toContain('b   ');
  });

  it('line shifts do not break scope detection', () => {
    const before = dedent`
      line1

      line2
      <!-- linter-disable: trailing-spaces -->
      a   
      <!-- linter-enable: trailing-spaces -->
      line3   
    `;
    const after = lint(before, ['trailing-spaces', 'consecutive-blank-lines']);
    expect(after).toContain('a   ');
    expect(after).toContain('<!-- linter-disable: trailing-spaces -->');
    expect(after).toContain('<!-- linter-enable: trailing-spaces -->');
  });

  it('nested enable removes from nearest scope', () => {
    const before = dedent`
      a   
      <!-- linter-disable: trailing-spaces -->
      b   
      <!-- linter-disable: trailing-spaces -->
      c   
      <!-- linter-enable: trailing-spaces -->
      d   
      <!-- linter-enable: trailing-spaces -->
      e   
    `;
    const after = lint(before, ['trailing-spaces']);
    expect(after).toContain('b   ');
    expect(after).toContain('c   ');
    expect(after).toContain('d   ');
    expect(after).not.toContain('e   ');
  });
});
EOF

import {getPositions, MDAstTypes} from '../src/utils/mdast';
import {getLinterDisableDirectives} from '../src/utils/linter-disable';
test('debug5', () => {
  const text = "`a\n<!-- linter-disable: trailing-spaces -->\nz`\n  b   \n";

# Insert helper functions before getBlockedLineNumbers
marker = "function getBlockedLineNumbers(text: string): Set<number> {"
helpers = '''function getLineStartOffsets(text: string): number[] {
  const lineStarts = [0];
  for (let i = 0; i < text.length; i++) {
    if (text[i] === '\\n') {
      lineStarts.push(i + 1);
    }
  }
  return lineStarts;
}

function getLineAtOffset(lineStarts: number[], offset: number): number {
  let low = 0;
  let high = lineStarts.length - 1;
  while (low < high) {
    const mid = Math.ceil((low + high) / 2);
    if (lineStarts[mid] <= offset) {
      low = mid;
    } else {
      high = mid - 1;
    }
  }
  return low;
}

function addRangeToLineSet(lineSet: Set<number>, lineStarts: number[], start: number, end: number): void {
  if (start >= end) {
    return;
  }
  const startLine = getLineAtOffset(lineStarts, start);
  const endLine = getLineAtOffset(lineStarts, Math.max(start, end - 1));
  for (let line = startLine; line <= endLine; line++) {
    lineSet.add(line);
  }
}

/**
 * Finds lines that are part of inline code spans (including multiline spans).
 * @param text The text to scan.
 * @return A set of zero based line numbers.
 */
function getInlineCodeLineNumbers(text: string): Set<number> {
  const lineNumbers = new Set<number>();
  const lineStarts = getLineStartOffsets(text);
  const backtickRunRegex = /`+/g;
  const runs: {start: number, end: number, length: number}[] = [];
  let match;
  while ((match = backtickRunRegex.exec(text)) !== null) {
    runs.push({start: match.index, end: match.index + match[0].length, length: match[0].length});
  }

  for (let i = 0; i < runs.length; i++) {
    const opening = runs[i];
    for (let j = i + 1; j < runs.length; j++) {
      if (runs[j].length === opening.length) {
        addRangeToLineSet(lineNumbers, lineStarts, opening.start, runs[j].end);
        i = j;
        break;
      }
    }
  }

  return lineNumbers;
}

/**
 * Finds lines that are part of inline math spans (including multiline spans).
 * @param text The text to scan.
 * @return A set of zero based line numbers.
 */
function getInlineMathLineNumbers(text: string): Set<number> {
  const lineNumbers = new Set<number>();
  const lineStarts = getLineStartOffsets(text);
  const dollarRunRegex = /\\$+/g;
  const runs: {start: number, end: number, length: number}[] = [];
  let match;
  while ((match = dollarRunRegex.exec(text)) !== null) {
    runs.push({start: match.index, end: match.index + match[0].length, length: match[0].length});
  }

  for (let i = 0; i < runs.length; i++) {
    const opening = runs[i];
    if (opening.length !== 1) {
      continue;
    }
    for (let j = i + 1; j < runs.length; j++) {
      if (runs[j].length === 1) {
        addRangeToLineSet(lineNumbers, lineStarts, opening.start, runs[j].end);
        i = j;
        break;
      }
    }
  }

  return lineNumbers;
}

# Rewrite getBlockedLineNumbers body to use addRangeToLineSet and add inline scanners
old_body = '''function getBlockedLineNumbers(text: string): Set<number> {
  const blockedLines = new Set<number>();

  const lineStarts = [0];
  for (let i = 0; i < text.length; i++) {
    if (text[i] === '\\n') {
      lineStarts.push(i + 1);
    }
  }

  const getLineAtOffset = (offset: number): number => {
    let low = 0;
    let high = lineStarts.length - 1;
    while (low < high) {
      const mid = Math.ceil((low + high) / 2);
      if (lineStarts[mid] <= offset) {
        low = mid;
      } else {
        high = mid - 1;
      }
    }
    return low;
  };

  const markRange = (start: number, end: number): void => {
    if (start >= end) {
      return;
    }
    const startLine = getLineAtOffset(start);
    const endLine = getLineAtOffset(Math.max(start, end - 1));
    for (let line = startLine; line <= endLine; line++) {
      blockedLines.add(line);
    }
  };

  const yamlMatch = text.match(yamlRegex);
  if (yamlMatch && yamlMatch.index !== undefined) {
    markRange(yamlMatch.index, yamlMatch.index + yamlMatch[0].length);
  }

  for (const position of getPositions(MDAstTypes.Code, text)) {
    markRange(position.start.offset, position.end.offset);
  }
  for (const position of getPositions(MDAstTypes.InlineCode, text)) {
    markRange(position.start.offset, position.end.offset);
  }
  for (const position of getPositions(MDAstTypes.Math, text)) {
    markRange(position.start.offset, position.end.offset);
  }
  for (const position of getPositions(MDAstTypes.InlineMath, text)) {
    markRange(position.start.offset, position.end.offset);
  }

  return blockedLines;
}'''

new_body = '''function getBlockedLineNumbers(text: string): Set<number> {
  const blockedLines = new Set<number>();
  const lineStarts = getLineStartOffsets(text);

  const markRange = (start: number, end: number): void => {
    addRangeToLineSet(blockedLines, lineStarts, start, end);
  };

  const yamlMatch = text.match(yamlRegex);
  if (yamlMatch && yamlMatch.index !== undefined) {
    markRange(yamlMatch.index, yamlMatch.index + yamlMatch[0].length);
  }

  for (const position of getPositions(MDAstTypes.Code, text)) {
    markRange(position.start.offset, position.end.offset);
  }
  for (const position of getPositions(MDAstTypes.InlineCode, text)) {
    markRange(position.start.offset, position.end.offset);
  }
  for (const position of getPositions(MDAstTypes.Math, text)) {
    markRange(position.start.offset, position.end.offset);
  }
  for (const position of getPositions(MDAstTypes.InlineMath, text)) {
    markRange(position.start.offset, position.end.offset);
  }

  for (const line of getInlineCodeLineNumbers(text)) {
    blockedLines.add(line);
  }
  for (const line of getInlineMathLineNumbers(text)) {
    blockedLines.add(line);
  }

  return blockedLines;
}'''

/**
 * Returns the zero based line numbers that are part of YAML frontmatter,
 * fenced or indented code blocks, inline code, or math blocks. Markers on
 * these lines are not recognized.
 * @param text The text to inspect.
 * @return A set of zero based line numbers to ignore.
 */
function getLineStartOffsets(text: string): number[] {
  const lineStarts = [0];
  for (let i = 0; i < text.length; i++) {
    if (text[i] === '\n') {
      lineStarts.push(i + 1);
    }
  }
  return lineStarts;
}

function getLineAtOffset(lineStarts: number[], offset: number): number {
  let low = 0;
  let high = lineStarts.length - 1;
  while (low < high) {
    const mid = Math.ceil((low + high) / 2);
    if (lineStarts[mid] <= offset) {
      low = mid;
    } else {
      high = mid - 1;
    }
  }
  return low;
}

function addRangeToLineSet(lineSet: Set<number>, lineStarts: number[], start: number, end: number): void {
  if (start >= end) {
    return;
  }
  const startLine = getLineAtOffset(lineStarts, start);
  const endLine = getLineAtOffset(lineStarts, Math.max(start, end - 1));
  for (let line = startLine; line <= endLine; line++) {
    lineSet.add(line);
  }
}

/**
 * Finds lines that are part of inline code spans (including multiline spans).
 * @param text The text to scan.
 * @return A set of zero based line numbers.
 */
function getInlineCodeLineNumbers(text: string): Set<number> {
  const lineNumbers = new Set<number>();
  const lineStarts = getLineStartOffsets(text);
  const backtickRunRegex = /`+/g;
  const runs: {start: number, end: number, length: number}[] = [];
  let match;
  while ((match = backtickRunRegex.exec(text)) !== null) {
    runs.push({start: match.index, end: match.index + match[0].length, length: match[0].length});
  }

  for (let i = 0; i < runs.length; i++) {
    const opening = runs[i];
    for (let j = i + 1; j < runs.length; j++) {
      if (runs[j].length === opening.length) {
        addRangeToLineSet(lineNumbers, lineStarts, opening.start, runs[j].end);
        i = j;
        break;
      }
    }
  }

  return lineNumbers;
}

 * Finds lines that are part of inline math spans (including multiline spans).
 * @param text The text to scan.
 * @return A set of zero based line numbers.
 */

COMMAND:
cd /app && cat docs/docs/usage/disabling-rules.md
OUTPUT:
# Ignoring or Disabling Rules

There are a couple of way to ignore rules in the Linter. These vary from settings in the plugin itself
to values in the YAML frontmatter, and a syntax to ignore rules for part or all of a file.

## Ignoring a Folder

There is a setting in the plugin for called `Folders to Ignore`. As the name suggests, this rule is meant
to allow users to specify folders that they do not want the linting rules to affect.
The values in the text box are expected to be folder paths from the base of the Obsidian vault.

![Setting for ignoring specific folders](../assets/folders-to-ignore.jpg)

For example, in the above image, the `templates` folder will be ignored when the Linter attempts to run its rules. Nested folders are also allowed as well.

## Ignoring Files via Regex

There is a setting in this plugin which allows you to be able to ignore files by providing a regex to match against.
If a file matches the provided regex, it will go ahead and ignore that file before it even lints the file.

![Setting for ignoring specific files via regex](../assets/files-to-ignore.jpg)

For example, in the above image you can see that Excalidraw files which end in `.exclidraw.md` are being ignored
using the regex `.*\.excalidraw\.md$`.

## File Specific Rule Disabling

There are times when there may be a need to disable a specific rule or rules for a particular file and there is no
desire to ignore all files in the folder where that file resides. In that case, there is the ability to disable a
rule or rules via the YAML frontmatter or ranged ignores.

### YAML Frontmatter

In the YAML frontmatter of a file, there is the ability to specify a list of rules to disable for the file using the key `disabled rules`.
Valid values for rules to disable are the rule aliases to disable specific rules or `all` to disable all rules for the file.

For example, the following would disable [capitalize headings](../settings/heading-rules.md#capitalize-headings) and [header increment](../settings/heading-rules.md#header-increment) for the entire file it is found in:
``` markdown
---
disabled rules: [capitalize-headings, header-increment]
---
```

The following disables all Linter rules for a file:
``` markdown
---
disabled rules: [all]
---
```

### Range Ignore

When there is a need to disable the Linter for part of a file, ranged ignores can be used. The syntax for a ranged ignore
is `<!-- linter-disable -->` or `%%linter-disable%%` with an optional `<!-- linter-enable -->` or `%%linter-disable%%` where you want the Linter to start back up with its linting.
Leaving off the ending of a range ignore will assume you want to ignore the file contents from the start of the range ignore to the end of the file. So be careful when not ending a range ignore.

!!! warning
    Ranged ignores only prevent the values in the ranged ignore from being linted. It *does not* prevent whitespace or other additions around the ranged ignore.

The following example shows how you would ignore just a part of a file:
``` markdown
Here is some text
<!-- linter-disable -->
                          This area will not be formatted
<!-- linter-enable -->
More content goes here...
%%linter-disable %%
                          This area will not be formatted
%%linter-enable%%
```

Here is another example that shows a ranged ignore without an ending indicator:
``` markdown
Here is some text
<!-- linter-disable -->
                          This area will not be formatted
This content is also not formatted either.
```

!!! info
    Paste rules are not affected by ranged ignores as that would require the copied text to have a ranged ignore in it.

cd /app && python3 - <<'PY'
path = 'docs/docs/usage/disabling-rules.md'
with open(path) as f:
    src = f.read()

addition = '''
### Scoped Per-Rule Ignore

Ranged ignores can also be scoped to specific rules and to individual lines. This allows you to keep the Linter active
for the rest of the file while preventing it from changing a particular rule (or rules) in a section of the file.

The markers work in both HTML comments and Obsidian comments:

``` markdown
<!-- linter-disable: rule-alias-1, rule-alias-2 -->
%% linter-disable: rule-alias-1, rule-alias-2 %%
```

To re-enable rules, use a matching `linter-enable` marker:

``` markdown
<!-- linter-enable: rule-alias-1, rule-alias-2 -->
%% linter-enable: rule-alias-1, rule-alias-2 %%
```

A `linter-disable` marker without a rule list disables all rules. A `linter-enable` marker without a rule list closes
the most recently opened disable scope. Disable scopes may be nested.

Rules can also be disabled for just the next line or the next `N` lines:

``` markdown
<!-- linter-disable-next-line: rule-alias-1 -->
<!-- linter-disable-next-n-lines: 5 rule-alias-1, rule-alias-2 -->
%% linter-disable-next-line: rule-alias-1 %%
%% linter-disable-next-n-lines: 5 rule-alias-1, rule-alias-2 %%
```

`linter-disable-next-line` and `linter-disable-next-n-lines` without a rule list disable all rules for the affected
lines. `N` must be a positive base-10 integer. If a requested line range extends past the end of the file it is
clamped to the end of the file.

Markers are only recognized when they appear on their own line (only spaces or tabs plus the marker). Markers inside
YAML frontmatter, fenced or indented code blocks, inline code, or math blocks are ignored. Marker lines themselves are
never modified by the Linter.

Here is an example that only disables `trailing-spaces` for part of a file:

``` markdown
Here is some text   
<!-- linter-disable: trailing-spaces -->
This area will keep its trailing spaces   
<!-- linter-enable: trailing-spaces -->
This area will have its trailing spaces removed   
```

'''
# Insert before the paste info note at the end
insertion_point = src.rstrip().rfind("\n!!! info")
if insertion_point == -1:
    src = src + addition
else:
    src = src[:insertion_point] + addition + src[insertion_point:]

with open(path, 'w') as f:
    f.write(src)
print("done")
PY

When there is a need to disable the Linter for part of a file, ranged ignores can be used. The syntax for a ranged ignore
is `<!-- linter-disable -->` or `%%linter-disable%%` with an optional `<!-- linter-enable -->` or `%%linter-disable%%` where you want the Linter to start back up with its linting.
Leaving off the ending of a range ignore will assume you want to ignore the file contents from the start of the range ignore to the end of the file. So be careful when not ending a range ignore.

!!! warning
    Ranged ignores only prevent the values in the ranged ignore from being linted. It *does not* prevent whitespace or other additions around the ranged ignore.

The following example shows how you would ignore just a part of a file:
``` markdown

                          This area will not be formatted
<!-- linter-enable -->
More content goes here...
%%linter-disable %%
                          This area will not be formatted
%%linter-enable%%
```

Here is another example that shows a ranged ignore without an ending indicator:
``` markdown
Here is some text
<!-- linter-disable -->
                          This area will not be formatted
This content is also not formatted either.
```

### Scoped Per-Rule Ignore

Ranged ignores can also be scoped to specific rules and to individual lines. This allows you to keep the Linter active
for the rest of the file while preventing it from changing a particular rule (or rules) in a section of the file.

The markers work in both HTML comments and Obsidian comments:

``` markdown

```

the most recently opened disable scope. Disable scopes may be nested.

Rules can also be disabled for just the next line or the next `N` lines:

```

`linter-disable-next-line` and `linter-disable-next-n-lines` without a rule list disable all rules for the affected
lines. `N` must be a positive base-10 integer. If a requested line range extends past the end of the file it is
clamped to the end of the file.

Markers are only recognized when they appear on their own line (only spaces or tabs plus the marker). Markers inside
YAML frontmatter, fenced or indented code blocks, inline code, or math blocks are ignored. Marker lines themselves are
never modified by the Linter.

Here is an example that only disables `trailing-spaces` for part of a file:

``` markdown

!!! info

cd /app && cat > __tests__/linter-disable.test.ts <<'EOF'
import {RulesRunner, createRunLinterRulesOptions} from '../src/rules-runner';
import {DEFAULT_SETTINGS, LinterSettings} from '../src/settings-data';
import {rules} from '../src/rules';
import {getLinterDisableDirectives, getLinterDisableSectionsInText, registerRuleAlias} from '../src/utils/linter-disable';
import '../src/rules-registry';
import {defaultMisspellings} from './common';
import dedent from 'ts-dedent';

const rulesRunner = new RulesRunner();

function lint(text: string, enabledRules: string[]): string {
  const settings = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(DEFAULT_SETTINGS)) as LinterSettings;
  settings.ruleConfigs = {};
  for (const rule of rules) {
    settings.ruleConfigs[rule.alias] = {};
  }
  for (const alias of enabledRules) {
    settings.ruleConfigs[alias] = {enabled: true};
  }
  const options = createRunLinterRulesOptions(text, null, 'en-gb', settings, defaultMisspellings());
  return rulesRunner.lintText(options);
}

describe('Linter Disable Markers', () => {
  describe('rule list normalization', () => {
    it('parses directives for both comment formats', () => {
      const text = dedent`
        <!-- linter-disable: trailing-spaces, capitalize-headings -->
        %% linter-disable-next-line: trailing-spaces %%
        %% linter-disable-next-n-lines: 2 trailing-spaces %%
      `;
      const directives = getLinterDisableDirectives(text);
      expect(directives.length).toBe(3);
      expect(directives[0].type).toBe('disable');
      expect([...directives[0].rules]).toEqual(['trailing-spaces', 'capitalize-headings']);
      expect(directives[1].type).toBe('disable-next-line');
      expect([...directives[1].rules]).toEqual(['trailing-spaces']);
      expect(directives[2].type).toBe('disable-next-n-lines');
      expect(directives[2].numLines).toBe(2);
    });

    it('normalizes case, removes duplicates, ignores empty entries and trailing commas', () => {
      const text = dedent`
        <!-- linter-disable: Trailing-Spaces, TRAILING-SPACES, , -->
      `;
      const directives = getLinterDisableDirectives(text);
      expect([...directives[0].rules]).toEqual(['trailing-spaces']);
    });

    it('ignores unknown rule aliases', () => {
      registerRuleAlias('trailing-spaces');
      const text = dedent`
        <!-- linter-disable: not-a-rule, trailing-spaces -->
      `;
      const directives = getLinterDisableDirectives(text);
      expect([...directives[0].rules]).toEqual(['trailing-spaces']);
    });

    it('has no effect when a rule list normalizes to empty', () => {
      const before = dedent`
        a   
        <!-- linter-disable: not-a-rule -->
        b   
      `;
      const after = lint(before, ['trailing-spaces']);
      expect(after).not.toContain('b   ');
    });

    it('treats a disable marker without a rule list as all rules', () => {
      const before = dedent`
        a   
        <!-- linter-disable -->
        b   
        <!-- linter-enable -->
        c   
      `;
      const after = lint(before, ['trailing-spaces']);
      expect(after).toContain('b   ');
      expect(after).not.toContain('c   ');
    });
  });

  describe('ranged scopes', () => {
    it('disables only the listed rule', () => {
      const before = dedent`
        Some text   
        <!-- linter-disable: trailing-spaces -->
        Ignored text   
        <!-- linter-enable: trailing-spaces -->
        More text   
      `;
      const after = lint(before, ['trailing-spaces', 'capitalize-headings']);
      expect(after).toContain('Ignored text   ');
      expect(after).toContain('<!-- linter-disable: trailing-spaces -->');
      expect(after).toContain('<!-- linter-enable: trailing-spaces -->');
      expect(after).not.toContain('Some text   ');
      expect(after).not.toContain('More text   ');
    });

    it('works with Obsidian comment markers', () => {
      const before = dedent`
        a   
        %% linter-disable: trailing-spaces %%
        b   
        %% linter-enable: trailing-spaces %%
        c   
      `;
      const after = lint(before, ['trailing-spaces']);
      expect(after).toContain('b   ');
      expect(after).not.toContain('c   ');
      expect(after).toContain('%% linter-disable: trailing-spaces %%');
      expect(after).toContain('%% linter-enable: trailing-spaces %%');
    });

    it('supports disabling all rules and re-enabling a specific rule within the scope', () => {
      const before = dedent`
        a   
        <!-- linter-disable -->
        b   
        <!-- linter-enable: trailing-spaces -->
        c   
        <!-- linter-enable -->
        d   
      `;
      const after = lint(before, ['trailing-spaces']);
      expect(after).toContain('b   ');
      expect(after).not.toContain('c   ');
      expect(after).not.toContain('d   ');
    });

    it('supports nested scopes with stack semantics', () => {
      const before = dedent`
        a   
        <!-- linter-disable -->
        b   
        <!-- linter-disable: trailing-spaces -->
        c   
        <!-- linter-enable -->
        d   
        <!-- linter-enable -->
        e   
      `;
      const after = lint(before, ['trailing-spaces']);
      expect(after).not.toContain('a   ');
      expect(after).toContain('b   ');
      expect(after).toContain('c   ');
      expect(after).toContain('d   ');
      expect(after).not.toContain('e   ');
    });

    it('closes only the nearest scope when enabling a specific rule', () => {
      const before = dedent`
        a   
        <!-- linter-disable: trailing-spaces -->
        b   
        <!-- linter-disable: trailing-spaces -->
        c   
        <!-- linter-enable: trailing-spaces -->
        d   
        <!-- linter-enable: trailing-spaces -->
        e   
      `;
      const after = lint(before, ['trailing-spaces']);
      expect(after).toContain('b   ');
      expect(after).toContain('c   ');
      expect(after).toContain('d   ');
      expect(after).not.toContain('e   ');
    });

    it('protects until end of file when a disable scope is not closed', () => {
      const before = dedent`
        a   
        <!-- linter-disable: trailing-spaces -->
        b   
        c   
      `;
      const after = lint(before, ['trailing-spaces']);
      expect(after).toContain('b   ');
      expect(after).toContain('c   ');
    });
  });

  describe('line scoped disables', () => {
    it('disables the next line', () => {
      const before = dedent`
        a   
        <!-- linter-disable-next-line: trailing-spaces -->
        b   
        c   
      `;
      const after = lint(before, ['trailing-spaces']);
      expect(after).toContain('b   ');
      expect(after).not.toContain('c   ');
    });

    it('disables the next N lines and clamps to end of file', () => {
      const before = dedent`
        a   
        <!-- linter-disable-next-n-lines: 5 trailing-spaces -->
        b   
        c   
      `;
      const after = lint(before, ['trailing-spaces']);
      expect(after).toContain('b   ');
      expect(after).toContain('c   ');
    });

    it('has no effect when there is no following line', () => {
      const before = `a   \n<!-- linter-disable-next-line: trailing-spaces -->\n`;
      const after = lint(before, ['trailing-spaces']);
      expect(after).not.toContain('a   ');
    });

    it('ignores non-positive or non-numeric N', () => {
      const before = dedent`
        a   
        <!-- linter-disable-next-n-lines: 0 trailing-spaces -->
        b   
        <!-- linter-disable-next-n-lines: -1 trailing-spaces -->
        c   
        <!-- linter-disable-next-n-lines: abc trailing-spaces -->
        d   
      `;
      const after = lint(before, ['trailing-spaces']);
      expect(after).not.toContain('b   ');
      expect(after).not.toContain('c   ');
      expect(after).not.toContain('d   ');
    });
  });

  describe('marker recognition', () => {
    it('only recognizes standalone markers', () => {
      const before = dedent`
        a <!-- linter-disable: trailing-spaces -->   
        b   
      `;
      const after = lint(before, ['trailing-spaces']);
      expect(after).not.toContain('b   ');
    });

    it('ignores markers inside YAML frontmatter', () => {
      const before = dedent`
        ---
        <!-- linter-disable: trailing-spaces -->
        ---
        a   
      `;
      const after = lint(before, ['trailing-spaces']);
      expect(after).not.toContain('a   ');
    });

    it('ignores markers inside fenced code blocks', () => {
      const before = dedent`
        a   
        \`\`\`
        <!-- linter-disable: trailing-spaces -->
        b   
        <!-- linter-enable: trailing-spaces -->
        \`\`\`
        c   
      `;
      const after = lint(before, ['trailing-spaces']);
      expect(after).toContain('<!-- linter-disable: trailing-spaces -->');
      expect(after).not.toContain('c   ');
    });

    it('ignores markers inside indented code blocks', () => {
      const before = dedent`
        a   
            <!-- linter-disable: trailing-spaces -->
        b   
      `;
      const after = lint(before, ['trailing-spaces']);
      expect(after).not.toContain('b   ');
    });

    it('ignores markers inside inline code', () => {
      const before = "`a\n<!-- linter-disable: trailing-spaces -->\nz`\n  b   \n";
      const after = lint(before, ['trailing-spaces']);
      expect(after).not.toContain('b   ');
    });

    it('ignores markers inside math blocks', () => {
      const before = dedent`
        $$
        <!-- linter-disable: trailing-spaces -->
        $$
        a   
      `;
      const after = lint(before, ['trailing-spaces']);
      expect(after).not.toContain('a   ');
    });
  });

  describe('marker line protection', () => {
    it('never modifies marker lines even for rules they do not disable', () => {
      const before = dedent`
        <!-- linter-disable: capitalize-headings -->   
        a   
      `;
      const after = lint(before, ['trailing-spaces']);
      expect(after).toContain('<!-- linter-disable: capitalize-headings -->   ');
    });

    it('never modifies marker lines for line scoped markers', () => {
      const before = dedent`
        <!-- linter-disable-next-line: trailing-spaces -->   
        a   
      `;
      const after = lint(before, ['trailing-spaces']);
      expect(after).toContain('<!-- linter-disable-next-line: trailing-spaces -->   ');
    });

    it('always includes marker lines in sections even when a rule is not disabled', () => {
      const text = dedent`
        <!-- linter-disable: trailing-spaces -->
        a
        <!-- linter-enable: trailing-spaces -->
        b
      `;
      const sections = getLinterDisableSectionsInText(text, 'capitalize-headings');
      expect(sections.length).toBe(2);
      expect(text.substring(sections[0].startIndex, sections[0].endIndex)).toContain('linter-disable');
      expect(text.substring(sections[1].startIndex, sections[1].endIndex)).toContain('linter-enable');
    });
  });

  describe('robustness', () => {
    it('re-detects scopes after other rules shift line numbers', () => {
      const before = dedent`
        line1

        line2
        <!-- linter-disable: trailing-spaces -->
        a   
        <!-- linter-enable: trailing-spaces -->
        line3   
      `;
      const after = lint(before, ['trailing-spaces', 'consecutive-blank-lines']);
      expect(after).toContain('a   ');
      expect(after).toContain('<!-- linter-disable: trailing-spaces -->');
      expect(after).toContain('<!-- linter-enable: trailing-spaces -->');
    });

    it('works when a rule is applied directly', () => {
      const {default: TrailingSpaces} = require('../src/rules/trailing-spaces') as typeof import('../src/rules/trailing-spaces');
      const rule = TrailingSpaces.getRule();
      const before = dedent`
        a   
        <!-- linter-disable: trailing-spaces -->
        b   
        <!-- linter-enable: trailing-spaces -->
        c   
      `;
      const after = rule.apply(before, {enabled: true});
      expect(after).toContain('b   ');
      expect(after).toContain('<!-- linter-disable: trailing-spaces -->');
    });
  });
});

cd /app && python3 - <<'PY'
path = 'src/utils/linter-disable.ts'
with open(path) as f:
    src = f.read()

src = src.replace(
    "import {yamlRegex} from './regex';",
    "import {codeBlockRegex, yamlRegex} from './regex';"
)

old = """  for (const position of getPositions(MDAstTypes.Code, text)) {
    markRange(position.start.offset, position.end.offset);
  }
"""
new = """  for (const position of getPositions(MDAstTypes.Code, text)) {
    markRange(position.start.offset, position.end.offset);
  }
  const codeBlockMatches = text.matchAll(codeBlockRegex);
  for (const match of codeBlockMatches) {
    markRange(match.index, match.index + match[0].length);
  }
"""
src = src.replace(old, new, 1)
with open(path, 'w') as f:
    f.write(src)
print("done")

              const scope = stack[index];
              if (scope.disabledRules !== null && !scope.disabledRules.has(rule)) {
                continue;
              }
              // This scope is currently disabling the rule. Record the range
              // up to (but not including) the enable marker.
              addRangeToRules(new Set([rule]), scope.startLine, directive.line - 1);

        }

    }

  for (const scope of stack) {
    addRangeToRules(scope.disabledRules, scope.startLine, totalLines - 1);

/**
 * Gets the sections of text that should be ignored for a given rule (or all
 * rules when no rule alias is supplied). The sections include all recognized
 * linter marker lines plus any lines disabled for the given rule.
 * @param text The text to inspect.
 * @param ruleAlias The rule being run, or undefined to return sections for
 * all rules.
 * @return The start and end indexes (end exclusive) of the sections to
 * ignore, in ascending order.
 */
export function getLinterDisableSectionsInText(text: string, ruleAlias?: string): {startIndex: number, endIndex: number}[] {
  const disabledLines = new Set<number>();
  const disabledLinesByRule = getDisabledLineNumbersByRule(text, ruleAlias);
  if (ruleAlias) {
    for (const line of disabledLinesByRule.get(ruleAlias) ?? []) {
      disabledLines.add(line);
    }
  } else {
    for (const lines of disabledLinesByRule.values()) {
      for (const line of lines) {
        disabledLines.add(line);
      }
    }
  }

  for (const line of getLinterDisableMarkerLines(text)) {
    disabledLines.add(line);
  }

  if (disabledLines.size === 0) {
    return [];
  }

  const lineStarts = [0];
  for (let i = 0; i < text.length; i++) {
    if (text[i] === '\n') {
      lineStarts.push(i + 1);
    }
  }

  const sortedLines = [...disabledLines].sort((a, b) => a - b);
  const sections: {startIndex: number, endIndex: number}[] = [];

  let rangeStart = sortedLines[0];
  let rangeEndLine = sortedLines[0];
  for (let i = 1; i < sortedLines.length; i++) {
    const line = sortedLines[i];
    if (line === rangeEndLine + 1) {
      rangeEndLine = line;
    } else {
      sections.push({
        startIndex: lineStarts[rangeStart],
        endIndex: rangeEndLine + 1 < lineStarts.length ? lineStarts[rangeEndLine + 1] : text.length,
      });
      rangeStart = line;
      rangeEndLine = line;
    }
  }
  sections.push({
    startIndex: lineStarts[rangeStart],
    endIndex: rangeEndLine + 1 < lineStarts.length ? lineStarts[rangeEndLine + 1] : text.length,
  });

  return sections;
}

/**
 * Alias of {@link getLinterDisableSectionsInText}.
 */
export function getAllLinterDisableSectionsInText(text: string, ruleAlias?: string): {startIndex: number, endIndex: number}[] {
  return getLinterDisableSectionsInText(text, ruleAlias).reverse();
}

/**
 * Alias of {@link getLinterDisableDirectives}.
 */
export function getLinterDisableCommentDirectives(text: string): LinterDisableDirective[] {
  return getLinterDisableDirectives(text);
}

/**
 * Alias of {@link getLinterDisableMarkerLines}.
 */
export function getLinterDisableCommentMarkersInText(text: string): number[] {
  return getLinterDisableMarkerLines(text);
}

     1	import {codeBlockRegex, yamlRegex} from './regex';
     2	import {getPositions, MDAstTypes} from './mdast';

     4	/**
     5	 * Known rule aliases. Populated as rules are registered.
     6	 */
     7	export const knownRuleAliases = new Set<string>();
     8	
     9	/**
    10	 * Registers a rule alias so that linter disable markers can validate
    11	 * whether a listed alias corresponds to an actual rule.
    12	 * @param alias The rule alias to register.
    13	 */
    14	export function registerRuleAlias(alias: string): void {
    15	  knownRuleAliases.add(alias);
    16	}
    17	
    18	/**
    19	 * A parsed linter disable/enable comment marker.
    20	 */
    21	export type LinterDisableDirective = {
    22	  /** The zero based line number the marker appears on. */
    23	  line: number,
    24	  /** The type of marker. */
    25	  type: 'disable' | 'enable' | 'disable-next-line' | 'disable-next-n-lines',
    26	  /**
    27	   * The rules affected by the marker.
    28	   * `null` means all rules.
    29	   * An empty set means the marker had no effect (for example an unknown
    30	   * rule list or an invalid number of lines).
    31	   */
    32	  rules: Set<string> | null,
    33	  /** For `disable-next-n-lines`, the number of lines to disable. */
    34	  numLines?: number,
    35	};
    36	
    37	type Scope = {
    38	  startLine: number,
    39	  disabledRules: Set<string> | null,
    40	};
    41	
    42	const htmlLinterMarkerRegex = /^[ \t]*<!-- linter-(disable-next-n-lines|disable-next-line|disable|enable)(.*?)-->[ \t]*\r?$/;
    43	const obsidianLinterMarkerRegex = /^[ \t]*%% linter-(disable-next-n-lines|disable-next-line|disable|enable)(.*?)%%[ \t]*\r?$/;
    44	
    45	/**
    46	 * Parses a comma separated rule list.
    47	 * @param ruleListText The raw text after a linter marker.
    48	 * @return The normalized set of rules, `null` when no rules were supplied
    49	 * (meaning all rules), or an empty set when the supplied rules normalized
    50	 * to nothing.
    51	 */
    52	function parseRuleList(ruleListText: string): Set<string> | null {
    53	  let text = ruleListText.trim();
    54	  if (text === '') {
    55	    return null;
    56	  }
    57	
    58	  if (text.startsWith(':')) {
    59	    text = text.substring(1).trim();
    60	    if (text === '') {
    61	      return null;
    62	    }
    63	  }
    64	
    65	  const rules = new Set<string>();
    66	  for (const rule of text.split(',')) {
    67	    const alias = rule.trim().toLowerCase();
    68	    if (alias === '') {
    69	      continue;
    70	    }
    71	    if (knownRuleAliases.size > 0 && !knownRuleAliases.has(alias)) {
    72	      continue;
    73	    }
    74	    rules.add(alias);
    75	  }
    76	
    77	  return rules;
    78	}
    79	
    80	/**
    81	 * Parses the argument of a `linter-disable-next-n-lines` marker.
    82	 * @param argument The raw argument text.
    83	 * @return The parsed number of lines and rule set, or null when the marker
    84	 * should have no effect.
    85	 */
    86	function parseNextNLinesArgument(argument: string): {numLines: number, rules: Set<string> | null} | null {
    87	  let text = argument.trim();
    88	  if (text === '') {
    89	    return null;
    90	  }
    91	  if (text.startsWith(':')) {
    92	    text = text.substring(1).trim();
    93	  }
    94	
    95	  const match = text.match(/^([0-9]+)(?::\s*|\s+)(.*)$/);
    96	  if (match) {
    97	    const numLines = Number(match[1]);
    98	    if (numLines <= 0) {
    99	      return null;
   100	    }
   101	    const rules = parseRuleList(match[2]);
   102	    if (rules !== null && rules.size === 0) {
   103	      return null;
   104	    }
   105	    return {numLines: numLines, rules: rules};
   106	  }
   107	
   108	  const onlyNumberMatch = text.match(/^([0-9]+)$/);
   109	  if (onlyNumberMatch) {
   110	    const numLines = Number(onlyNumberMatch[1]);
   111	    if (numLines <= 0) {
   112	      return null;
   113	    }
   114	    return {numLines: numLines, rules: null};
   115	  }
   116	
   117	  return null;
   118	}
   119	
   120	/**
   121	 * Returns the zero based line numbers that are part of YAML frontmatter,
   122	 * fenced or indented code blocks, inline code, or math blocks. Markers on
   123	 * these lines are not recognized.
   124	 * @param text The text to inspect.
   125	 * @return A set of zero based line numbers to ignore.
   126	 */
   127	function getLineStartOffsets(text: string): number[] {
   128	  const lineStarts = [0];
   129	  for (let i = 0; i < text.length; i++) {
   130	    if (text[i] === '\n') {
   131	      lineStarts.push(i + 1);
   132	    }
   133	  }
   134	  return lineStarts;
   135	}
   136	
   137	function getLineAtOffset(lineStarts: number[], offset: number): number {
   138	  let low = 0;
   139	  let high = lineStarts.length - 1;
   140	  while (low < high) {
   141	    const mid = Math.ceil((low + high) / 2);
   142	    if (lineStarts[mid] <= offset) {
   143	      low = mid;
   144	    } else {
   145	      high = mid - 1;
   146	    }
   147	  }
   148	  return low;
   149	}
   150	
   151	function addRangeToLineSet(lineSet: Set<number>, lineStarts: number[], start: number, end: number): void {
   152	  if (start >= end) {
   153	    return;
   154	  }
   155	  const startLine = getLineAtOffset(lineStarts, start);
   156	  const endLine = getLineAtOffset(lineStarts, Math.max(start, end - 1));
   157	  for (let line = startLine; line <= endLine; line++) {
   158	    lineSet.add(line);
   159	  }
   160	}
   161	
   162	/**
   163	 * Finds lines that are part of inline code spans (including multiline spans).
   164	 * @param text The text to scan.
   165	 * @return A set of zero based line numbers.
   166	 */
   167	function getInlineCodeLineNumbers(text: string): Set<number> {
   168	  const lineNumbers = new Set<number>();
   169	  const lineStarts = getLineStartOffsets(text);
   170	  const backtickRunRegex = /`+/g;
   171	  const runs: {start: number, end: number, length: number}[] = [];
   172	  let match;
   173	  while ((match = backtickRunRegex.exec(text)) !== null) {
   174	    runs.push({start: match.index, end: match.index + match[0].length, length: match[0].length});
   175	  }
   176	
   177	  for (let i = 0; i < runs.length; i++) {
   178	    const opening = runs[i];
   179	    for (let j = i + 1; j < runs.length; j++) {
   180	      if (runs[j].length === opening.length) {
   181	        addRangeToLineSet(lineNumbers, lineStarts, opening.start, runs[j].end);
   182	        i = j;
   183	        break;
   184	      }
   185	    }
   186	  }
   187	
   188	  return lineNumbers;
   189	}
   190	
   191	
   192	function getBlockedLineNumbers(text: string): Set<number> {
   193	  const blockedLines = new Set<number>();
   194	  const lineStarts = getLineStartOffsets(text);
   195	
   196	  const markRange = (start: number, end: number): void => {
   197	    addRangeToLineSet(blockedLines, lineStarts, start, end);
   198	  };
   199	
   200	  const yamlMatch = text.match(yamlRegex);
   201	  if (yamlMatch && yamlMatch.index !== undefined) {
   202	    markRange(yamlMatch.index, yamlMatch.index + yamlMatch[0].length);
   203	  }
   204	
   205	  for (const position of getPositions(MDAstTypes.Code, text)) {
   206	    markRange(position.start.offset, position.end.offset);
   207	  }
   208	  const codeBlockMatches = text.matchAll(codeBlockRegex);
   209	  for (const match of codeBlockMatches) {
   210	    markRange(match.index, match.index + match[0].length);
   211	  }
   212	  for (const position of getPositions(MDAstTypes.InlineCode, text)) {
   213	    markRange(position.start.offset, position.end.offset);
   214	  }
   215	  for (const position of getPositions(MDAstTypes.Math, text)) {
   216	    markRange(position.start.offset, position.end.offset);
   217	  }
   218	
   219	  for (const line of getInlineCodeLineNumbers(text)) {
   220	    blockedLines.add(line);
   221	  }
   222	
   223	  return blockedLines;
   224	}

 This content is also not formatted either.
 ```
 
+### Scoped Per-Rule Ignore
+
+Ranged ignores can also be scoped to specific rules and to individual lines. This allows you to keep the Linter active
+for the rest of the file while preventing it from changing a particular rule (or rules) in a section of the file.
+
+The markers work in both HTML comments and Obsidian comments:
+
+``` markdown
+<!-- linter-disable: rule-alias-1, rule-alias-2 -->
+%% linter-disable: rule-alias-1, rule-alias-2 %%
+```
+
+To re-enable rules, use a matching `linter-enable` marker:
+
+``` markdown
+<!-- linter-enable: rule-alias-1, rule-alias-2 -->
+%% linter-enable: rule-alias-1, rule-alias-2 %%
+```
+
+A `linter-disable` marker without a rule list disables all rules. A `linter-enable` marker without a rule list closes
+the most recently opened disable scope. Disable scopes may be nested.
+
+Rules can also be disabled for just the next line or the next `N` lines:
+
+``` markdown
+<!-- linter-disable-next-line: rule-alias-1 -->
+<!-- linter-disable-next-n-lines: 5 rule-alias-1, rule-alias-2 -->
+%% linter-disable-next-line: rule-alias-1 %%
+%% linter-disable-next-n-lines: 5 rule-alias-1, rule-alias-2 %%
+```
+
+`linter-disable-next-line` and `linter-disable-next-n-lines` without a rule list disable all rules for the affected
+lines. `N` must be a positive base-10 integer. If a requested line range extends past the end of the file it is
+clamped to the end of the file.
+
+Markers are only recognized when they appear on their own line (only spaces or tabs plus the marker). Markers inside
+YAML frontmatter, fenced or indented code blocks, inline code, or math blocks are ignored. Marker lines themselves are
+never modified by the Linter.
+
+Here is an example that only disables `trailing-spaces` for part of a file:
+
+``` markdown
+Here is some text   
+<!-- linter-disable: trailing-spaces -->
+This area will keep its trailing spaces   
+<!-- linter-enable: trailing-spaces -->
+This area will have its trailing spaces removed   
+```
+
+
 !!! info
     Paste rules are not affected by ranged ignores as that would require the copied text to have a ranged ignore in it.

+function replaceLinterDisableSections(text: string, placeholder: string, ruleAlias?: string): [string[], string] {
+  const linterDisableSections = getLinterDisableSectionsInText(text, ruleAlias);
+
+  const replacedSections: string[] = new Array(linterDisableSections.length);

+  }
+
+  return [replacedSections, text];

describe('Linter Disable Markers - syntax variants', () => {
  it('supports colon after next-n-lines N', () => {
    const text = dedent`
      <!-- linter-disable-next-n-lines: 5: trailing-spaces -->
    `;
    const directives = getLinterDisableDirectives(text);
    expect(directives[0].type).toBe('disable-next-n-lines');
    expect(directives[0].numLines).toBe(5);
    expect([...directives[0].rules]).toEqual(['trailing-spaces']);
  });

  it('supports disable-next-line without a colon before the rule list', () => {
    const text = dedent`
      %% linter-disable-next-line trailing-spaces %%
    `;
    const directives = getLinterDisableDirectives(text);
    expect(directives[0].type).toBe('disable-next-line');
    expect([...directives[0].rules]).toEqual(['trailing-spaces']);
  });

  it('supports disable-next-n-lines without a colon before N', () => {
    const text = dedent`
      <!-- linter-disable-next-n-lines 3 trailing-spaces -->
    `;
    const directives = getLinterDisableDirectives(text);
    expect(directives[0].type).toBe('disable-next-n-lines');
    expect(directives[0].numLines).toBe(3);
    expect([...directives[0].rules]).toEqual(['trailing-spaces']);
  });

  it('supports trailing whitespace after a marker', () => {
    const before = dedent`
      a   
      <!-- linter-disable: trailing-spaces -->   
      b   
      <!-- linter-enable: trailing-spaces -->   
      c   
    `;
    const after = lint(before, ['trailing-spaces']);
    expect(after).toContain('b   ');
    expect(after).not.toContain('c   ');
  });
});

import {RulesRunner, createRunLinterRulesOptions} from '../src/rules-runner';
import {DEFAULT_SETTINGS, LinterSettings} from '../src/settings-data';
import {rules} from '../src/rules';
import '../src/rules-registry';
import {defaultMisspellings} from './common';
import dedent from 'ts-dedent';

const runner = new RulesRunner();
function lint(text: string) {
  const settings = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(DEFAULT_SETTINGS)) as LinterSettings;
  settings.ruleConfigs = {};
  for (const rule of rules) settings.ruleConfigs[rule.alias] = {};
  settings.ruleConfigs['trailing-spaces'] = {enabled: true};
  return runner.lintText(createRunLinterRulesOptions(text, null, 'en-gb', settings, defaultMisspellings()));
}
test('sanity', () => {
  const before = dedent`
    a   
    <!-- linter-disable: trailing-spaces -->
    b   
    <!-- linter-enable: trailing-spaces -->
    c   
    <!-- linter-disable: trailing-spaces -->
    d   
    <!-- linter-enable: trailing-spaces -->
    e   
  `;
  const after = lint(before);
  console.log(JSON.stringify(after));
  expect(after).toContain('b   ');
  expect(after).toContain('d   ');
  expect(after).not.toContain('c   ');
  expect(after).not.toContain('e   ');
});

      %%linter-enable: trailing-spaces%%
    `;

    Ranged ignores are applied while linting a file. Paste rules run on the text being pasted, so a ranged ignore
    in the pasted text can affect paste rules in the same way it affects other rules.

Add support for HTML and Obsidian comment markers that disable rules for
a section of a file, for the next line, or for the next N lines. Markers
are only recognized on standalone lines outside YAML frontmatter, code
blocks, inline code, and math blocks. Marker lines are always preserved.

# Add alias after getAllLinterDisableSectionsInText
old = """/**
 * Alias of {@link getLinterDisableDirectives}.
 */
export function getLinterDisableCommentDirectives"""
new = """/**
 * Alias of {@link getLinterDisableSectionsInText}.
 * @param {string} text The text to inspect.
 * @param {string=} ruleAlias The rule being run, or undefined to return sections for all rules.
 * @return {Object[]} The start and end indexes (end exclusive) of the sections to ignore.
 */
export function getLinterDisableSections(text: string, ruleAlias?: string): {startIndex: number, endIndex: number}[] {
  return getLinterDisableSectionsInText(text, ruleAlias);
}

/**
 * Alias of {@link getLinterDisableDirectives}.
 */
export function getLinterDisableCommentDirectives"""
src = src.replace(old, new)

    f.write(src)
print("done")

import {getLinterDisableSectionsInText} from '../src/utils/linter-disable';
import dedent from 'ts-dedent';
test('no registration', () => {
  const text = dedent`
    a
    <!-- linter-disable -->
    b
    c
  `;
  const sections = getLinterDisableSectionsInText(text);
  console.log(JSON.stringify(sections));
  expect(sections.length).toBe(1);
  const protectedText = text.substring(sections[0].startIndex, sections[0].endIndex);
  expect(protectedText).toContain('b');
  expect(protectedText).toContain('c');
});
